class op9_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 32, p2: 64}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index1 = p0 + 7*(ModularIndexing(p2, 1, 32))
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2)) + 32
    index4 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf6', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_2, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(64, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_6)
        sin = ops.sin(load_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(sin, constant_5)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(where, mul_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, mul_3)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf9', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index3')
        load = ops.load('buf6', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')
        load = ops.load('buf6', get_index)
        return load

class op34_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 32, p2: 64}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index1 = p0 + 7*(ModularIndexing(p2, 1, 32))
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2)) + 32
    index4 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf31', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_2, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(64, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_6)
        sin = ops.sin(load_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(sin, constant_5)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(where, mul_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, mul_3)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf34', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index3')
        load = ops.load('buf31', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')
        load = ops.load('buf31', get_index)
        return load

class op59_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 32, p2: 64}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index1 = p0 + 7*(ModularIndexing(p2, 1, 32))
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2)) + 32
    index4 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf56', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)

class op134_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 32, p2: 64}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index1 = p0 + 7*(ModularIndexing(p2, 1, 32))
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2)) + 32
    index4 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf131', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_2, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(64, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_6)
        sin = ops.sin(load_2)

        mul_2 = ops.mul(sin, constant_5)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(where, mul_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, mul_3)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf134', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index3')
        load = ops.load('buf131', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')

        return load

buf2_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[32, 7], stride=[7, 1])

class op184_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 32, p2: 64}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index1 = p0 + 7*(ModularIndexing(p2, 1, 32))
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2)) + 32
    index4 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf181', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_2, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(64, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_6)
        sin = ops.sin(load_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(sin, constant_5)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(where, mul_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, mul_3)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf184', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index3')
        load = ops.load('buf181', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')
        load = ops.load('buf181', get_index)
        return load

    def body(self, ops):

        load = ops.load('buf182', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)

        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)

        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_6)
        sin = ops.sin(load_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(sin, constant_5)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(where, mul_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, mul_3)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf185', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index3')
        load = ops.load('buf182', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')
        load = ops.load('buf182', get_index)
        return load

    index4 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))

        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)

        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)

        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)

        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)

        store = ops.store('buf209', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store

        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')

class op234_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 32, p2: 64}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index1 = p0 + 7*(ModularIndexing(p2, 1, 32))
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2)) + 32
    index4 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf231', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')

class op309_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 32, p2: 64}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index1 = p0 + 7*(ModularIndexing(p2, 1, 32))
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2)) + 32
    index4 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf306', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_2, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(64, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_6)
        sin = ops.sin(load_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(sin, constant_5)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(where, mul_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, mul_3)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf309', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index3')
        load = ops.load('buf306', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')
        load = ops.load('buf306', get_index)
        return load

buf2_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[32, 7], stride=[7, 1])
buf356_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[7, 2048], stride=[2048, 1])
buf356_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[7, 2048], stride=[2048, 1])
buf359_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1, 32, 7, 64], stride=[14336, 64, 2048, 1])
class op359_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 32, p2: 64}
    index0 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index1 = p0 + 7*(ModularIndexing(p2, 1, 32))
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2)) + 32
    index4 = 2048*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf356', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_2, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(64, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_6)
        sin = ops.sin(load_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(sin, constant_5)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(where, mul_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, mul_3)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf359', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index3')
        load = ops.load('buf356', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')
        load = ops.load('buf356', get_index)
        return load

class op360_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 8, p2: 64}
    index0 = 512*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index1 = p0 + 7*(ModularIndexing(p2, 1, 32))
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 512*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2)) + 32
    index4 = 512*p0 + 64*p1 + ((p2 - 32))
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf357', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_1)
        cos = ops.cos(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant_1)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_2)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_2, constant_3)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(64, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(ge_1, 0.0)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf2', get_index_6)
        sin = ops.sin(load_2)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(sin, constant_5)
        mul_3 = ops.mul(where, mul_2)
        add = ops.add(mul_1, mul_3)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index0')
        store = ops.store('buf360', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index3')
        load = ops.load('buf357', get_index)
        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index4')
        load = ops.load('buf357', get_index)
        return load

        constant = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(cos, constant)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(load, mul)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)

        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)

        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock1, masked_subblock2)

        store = ops.store('buf384', get_index_7, add, None)
        return store

        neg = ops.neg(load)
        return neg
    def masked_subblock2(self, ops):
